# Agents · S · part 1

959 entries, S-0001 to sa-map-generate-controls. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **S-0001**: First story of E-0001. The repository was assembled on 2026-09-23 from eleven per-component repositories but the move stopped there. This story makes it one checkable monorepo: scaffolding committed, design records folded into the system-flow layout (ADR-0106), `integration` imported, template locked, private remote created. ([bytepunx/authstar](https://github.com/bytepunx/authstar/blob/HEAD/wip/archive/agents/S-0001.md))
- **S-0001**: The operator asked for an Elixir client for signet that is SPIFFE/SPIRE compatible. The Go client is the reference design; the Erlang folder's README records that Buf's registry has no BEAM plugin. This story delivers the connection layer (generated stubs, admin access, SPIFFE workload access); S-0002 and S-0003 carry restart coordination and SOPS encryption. ([bytepunx/signet](https://github.com/bytepunx/signet/blob/HEAD/wip/archive/agents/S-0001.md))
- **S-0001** ([bytepunx/system-flow](https://github.com/bytepunx/system-flow/blob/HEAD/wip/archive/agents/S-0001.md))
- **S-0002**: Second story of E-0001. Per-component CI was orphaned by the monorepo move (GitHub Actions ignores `.github` below the root) and the root scripts were template stubs. ([bytepunx/authstar](https://github.com/bytepunx/authstar/blob/HEAD/wip/archive/agents/S-0002.md))

- **s-alembic**: Alembic migration lifecycle subagent. Invoked via Task by p-coder-batch-mode, p-coder-fix-mode, p-test-runner, and p-devops. Owns the full migration lifecycle: generate (autogenerate + TimescaleDB hypertable augmentation), apply-test (migrate test_pheidipp), pending-changes-check (verify migration captures all ORM changes), and apply-prod (migrate pheidipp). The coder never writes migration files directly — this subagent owns that end-to-end. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-alembic.md) · ★1)
- **s-code-explorer**: Read-only codebase resolver, invoked only via Task by p-test-architect (Test Architect Mode). Takes a caller-supplied, already-resolved file list (a test stage's capability group) and returns a condensed Brief: current file content, signatures, existing sibling implementations, registration points, and fixture matches, plus a Verification/Confidence header. Never writes or edits anything. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-code-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **s-code-structure-explorer**: AST-based code structure resolver. Invoked via Task by p-coder-batch-mode, p-coder-fix-mode, p-implementation-architect, p-implementation-resolver, and p-implementation-validator. Takes a file path or module name and returns structural information: classes, functions, imports, and relationships. Never writes or edits. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-code-structure-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **s-contract-verifier**: Event and invariant contract verification subagent. Invoked via Task by p-coder-batch-mode, p-coder-fix-mode, p-implementation-architect, p-implementation-resolver, p-test-architect, or p-implementation-validator. Takes an entity or event name and verifies its contracts against the architecture. Never writes or edits anything. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-contract-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **s-devops-ops**: Docker services lifecycle subagent. Invoked via Task by p-devops and p-test-runner. Mechanical docker-compose lifecycle: services-up, services-check, build-verify. No judgment, no migration logic, no test execution, no config file authoring (that's s-infra-config-editor). Runs scripts and reports pass/fail only. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-devops-ops.md) · ★1)
- **s-diagnostics-fixer**: Invoked via Task by p-coder-batch-mode, p-coder-fix-mode, or p-test-architect at the end of implementation or test generation, or directly by the user for full-repo diagnostic cleanup. In plan-based mode, takes a plan_id and a file list; in full-repo mode, takes neither and scans the entire workspace per pyrightconfig. Runs the typecheck → cluster → fix → recheck loop until zero errors or max_iterations, and returns a summary report. Uses a cheap model — diagnostics fixing is mechanical, not architectural. Does not run tests, migrations, or builds. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-diagnostics-fixer.md) · ★1)
- **s-doc-explorer**: Read-only documentation corpus resolver, invoked only via Task by p-implementation-architect, p-implementation-resolver, p-release-strategy-architect, s-vision-and-architect-author, or p-technical-advisor. Takes a caller-supplied task description and concept list and returns a condensed Brief: the relevant pages from architecture, vision, release-plan, and ADR corpora — nothing more. Does not perform open-ended discovery, does not decide relevance beyond what the caller named, and never writes or edits anything. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-doc-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **s-documentation**: Maintains per-folder README.md documentation for app/ and tests/. Four modes, never combined: Incremental (invoked by p-coder-batch-mode or p-test-architect), Baseline (bootstraps READMEs), Cleanup (strips comment noise), Summarize (moves bloated inline docs to READMEs, compacts docstrings). ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-documentation.md) · ★1)
- **s-expo-builder**: Writes and changes s-expo code strictly test-first under s-expo-mode (ub-mode) discipline — failing test, seen failing, smallest passing change, refactor while green. Use for Phase 2 of s-expo-dev, for fixing findings from s-expo-quality or s-expo-reviewer, and when asked to "implement", "write the code", "make this test pass". ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/s-expo-builder.md))
- **s-expo-builder**: ../../.agents/agents/s-expo-builder.md ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s-expo-builder.md))
- **s-expo-docs-keeper**: Keeps s-expo's project memory and docs true after a change — glossary terms, decisions, lessons and the work log in docs/memory/, plus any docs/ page, AGENTS.md or README section the change made false. Use for Phase 5 of s-expo-dev, and when asked "update the docs", "remember this", "record this decision", "add to memory". ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/s-expo-docs-keeper.md))
- **s-expo-docs-keeper**: ../../.agents/agents/s-expo-docs-keeper.md ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s-expo-docs-keeper.md))
- **s-expo-planner**: ../../.agents/agents/s-expo-planner.md ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s-expo-planner.md))
- **s-expo-planner**: Read-only planner for s-expo code changes. Classifies the change, maps the files and layers it touches, names the first failing test and orders the work into small verifiable units. Use at the start of any feature, fix or refactor (Phase 1 of s-expo-dev), or when asked "plan this", "how would we build X", "what does this touch". ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/s-expo-planner.md))
- **s-expo-quality**: Runs s-expo's quality gates and reports numbers — check-all (lint, types, translations, Jest, hook tests), CRAP via crapper, duplication via dryer, mutation via mutator on changed files. Does not change code. Use for Phase 3 of s-expo-dev, before a pull request, or when asked "run the gates", "check quality", "CRAP report", "mutation test". ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/s-expo-quality.md))
- **s-expo-quality**: ../../.agents/agents/s-expo-quality.md ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s-expo-quality.md))
- **s-expo-reviewer**: Read-only code reviewer for s-expo diffs. Checks correctness first, then s-expo-mode principles, project conventions (dependency direction, routes as re-exports, translations, Uniwind tokens), test honesty and the quality report. Use for Phase 4 of s-expo-dev, or when asked "review this", "review my branch", "is this ready to merge". ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/s-expo-reviewer.md))
- **s-expo-reviewer**: ../../.agents/agents/s-expo-reviewer.md ([tiagoolivv/s.expo](https://github.com/tiagoolivv/s.expo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/s-expo-reviewer.md))
- **s-history-explorer**: Read-only historical artifact scanner. Available for on-demand invocation by any agent needing historical report context (currently not wired into any standard pipeline — invokers should add `s-history-explorer: allow` and an invocation template). Takes a caller-supplied task description and scope (plan ID, phase, or domain) and returns a condensed Brief: raw excerpts from prior validation reports, DevOps reports, diagnostics reports, implementation plans, and manifests that are relevant to the caller's task. Does not detect patterns, does not make routing decisions, and never writes or edits… ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-history-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **s-impact-analyzer**: Blast-radius and dependency analysis subagent. Invoked via Task by p-coder-batch-mode, p-coder-fix-mode, p-implementation-architect, and p-implementation-resolver. Takes a concept name and returns a structured impact report: what depends on it, what it depends on, and what would break if changed. Never writes or edits anything. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-impact-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **s-index-health-guard**: Index health checker and selective refresher. Invoked via Task by implementation-pipeline agents at session start or when results seem stale (p-coder-batch-mode, p-coder-fix-mode, p-implementation-architect, p-implementation-resolver, p-implementation-validator, p-test-architect, p-devops). Checks index health and refreshes only the domains relevant to the caller. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-index-health-guard.md) · ★1)
- **s-manifest-manager**: Manifest write subagent. Invoked by p-devops for promotion operations (promote-file, release-promote) and by p-test-architect for phase file authoring (write-phase). Does not run tests — only writes manifest files. Owns the split/collapse algorithm. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-manifest-manager.md) · ★1)
- **s-state-explorer**: Read-only codebase registry resolver, invoked only via Task by p-implementation-architect, p-implementation-resolver, p-implementation-validator, or p-consistency-validator. Takes a caller-supplied domain description or entity list and returns a condensed Brief: the current registry of what exists in that domain — entities, services, repositories, routes, registrations, event producers, transaction boundaries, and which code files map to each entity. Does not perform open-ended discovery, does not resolve file content (that is s-code-explorer's job), and never writes or edits anything. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-state-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **s-test-analyzer**: Test failure analysis and routing subagent. Invoked via Task by p-test-runner when tests fail. Receives a pre-extracted failure summary (the Juice — p-test-runner's mechanical compaction of raw pytest output, NOT the raw output itself). Classifies failures into root causes, assigns owners (p-coder-fix-mode, p-tester-fix-mode, p-infra-fixer, p-implementation-resolver). Writes the devops report to reports/&lt;plan-id&gt;_devops.md via `write`. Returns a short RC bullet summary to p-test-runner (never the full report text). Does NOT apply fixes — all fix execution is owned by the routed agent (p-infra… ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-test-analyzer.md) · ★1)
- **s-test-executor**: Mechanical test execution subagent. Invoked via Task by p-test-runner, p-coder-fix-mode, and p-tester-fix-mode. Runs pytest via scripts/run-tests.sh against explicit selectors, captures output to /tmp/, and returns PASS or FAIL with the verbatim FAILED/ERROR lines (the Juice — each line carries the exception reason, extracted from the JUnit XML by the script's --- JUICE START/END --- section). Does NOT diagnose, classify, read the manifest, or write reports. The most minimal subagent in the ecosystem. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-test-executor.md) · ★1)
- **s-vision-and-architect-author**: Invoked as a subagent by p-technical-advisor to update architecture and vision documentation in response to implementation changes. Also usable standalone for direct vision/architecture authoring. The only agent allowed to modify docs/architecture/ and docs/vision/. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-vision-and-architect-author.md) · ★1)
- **s-web-researcher**: Web research subagent. Invoked via Task by any agent that needs external knowledge — library docs, GitHub issues, Stack Overflow, changelogs. Receives a research question + context, searches the web, and returns a condensed factual brief with source URLs. Does NOT make decisions, edit files, or access the codebase. Returns facts only — the caller decides what to do with them. ([dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend](https://github.com/dromeas-developer/pheidipp-backend/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/s-web-researcher.md) · ★1)

- **S1-landscape-draft**: Our agent is Claude Opus 5.5 in Claude Code. It has **no video or audio input**: text, images and PDF only, with every image Read clamped to 2000 px per side, and the API capped at 600 images per request (MEASURED and DOCUMENTED, `HP:43-45`). So every product below reaches it through a tool. This draft merges the ten axis reports (R1-R10), the completeness critic (C1) and an adversarial check of a ([renchris/claude-infrastructure](https://github.com/renchris/claude-infrastructure/blob/HEAD/docs/research/video-understanding-for-agents-landscape-2026-10-24/agents/S1-landscape-draft.md) · ★1)
- **S1-method-draft**: **What this is.** A step-by-step method for frequent, founder-led demo launches. It is distilled from Farza Majeed's YC interview, then checked three ways: against his own launch videos measured frame by frame, against what he has said elsewhere, and against outside evidence. This is the stage-1 research deliverable; stage 2 turns it into a skill (§8). Slot S1 wrote this final pass from S0's draft ([renchris/claude-infrastructure](https://github.com/renchris/claude-infrastructure/blob/HEAD/docs/research/farza-launch-video-method-2026-10-24/agents/S1-method-draft.md) · ★1)

- **sa**: 架構師（SA）。兩種模式的架構判斷——對一段改動做審查（模組邊界、接縫位置、不變式歸屬、單一事實來源、失敗模式、多餘的抽象與過渡期程式的去留，與專案詞彙表的相容性），或不看 diff 掃整個 codebase 找值得加深的模組。當使用者要求架構審查、質疑某個設計是否放對位置、要評估一次改動有沒有把耦合留在錯的地方，或想知道這個 codebase 接下來該改善哪裡時使用。它只提出判斷與取捨，不改任何檔案；行級缺陷與清理交給 code review。 ([gunter1020/devteam](https://github.com/gunter1020/devteam/blob/HEAD/agents/sa.md) · ★1)
- **sa**: 售前解决方案工程师角色总入口（Role Meta-Agent）— 你只需要一句自然语言告诉我你要做什么， 我会拆解成 3-8 步执行计划，展示给你确认后自动依次调用具体命令。 专注端到端 7 模块流水线（M1 输入接入 → M2 客户调研 → M3 方案拆解 → M4 总体方案架构 → M5 POC/实施/RFP → M6 报价宣讲 → M7 归档）。 服务对外客户售前项目场景，与服务内部 PRD 场景的 @pm 互补。 ([yf-hash/WorkAgent](https://github.com/yf-hash/WorkAgent/blob/HEAD/agents/sa.md) · ★1)
